## Rental Income and Gross Annual Yield

Estimates use advertised monthly base rents, not signed leases. Utility/service charges are screened from listing text; inclusive or unclear prices are excluded. Verify terms before investing. Comparables require the same layout and municipality, floor area within 25%, and at least 3 unique listings. Monthly rent uses median rent per m2 scaled to the purchase property's area. The range is the minimum-to-maximum comparable rate scaled to that area, not a confidence interval. Condition, furnishings and lease terms are not adjusted.

**Gross annual yield = monthly rent x 12 / asking purchase price x 100.** This is not net ROI: vacancy, maintenance, insurance, taxes, transaction costs and financing are excluded.
